# KTM 85 SX 17/14 2025: aftermarket parts

3846 listings whose stated fitment names this bike: 251 researched parts (with what they change and rider findings), 1359 dealer and retailer listings specific to this bike, and 2236 universal retailer listings (oil, grips, batteries and the like, counted below; every listing is in [parts.csv](../parts.csv)). Research candidates: check fitment on the maker's page before relying on a row. Back to [the family catalog](../CATALOG.md).
